# spice-netlist

# 상위 cell로가면서 연결이 잘못된 Case.
xiC0CMRDEC_G3 
MA3H_RDP MA3H_WRP MA3L_RDP MA3L_WRP MA012<0> MA012<1> MA012<2> MA012<3> MA012<4> MA012<5> MA456<1> MA456<3> MA456<6> MA456<7> MR16WPB MR16WPD MR17RP MR17WP MR18RP MR18WP MR21RPB MR21RPD MR64RPB MR64RPD MR65RPB MR65RPD MR105WPB MR105WPD MR106RPB MR106RPD MR107RPB MR107RPD MR108RPB MR108RPD MR120RPB MR120RPD MR121WPB MR121WPD MR122RPB MR122RPD MRG3RDP MR_AUTOMOTIVE_EN MR_CATENB MR_CSTENB VPERI VPERI_ZPG VSS VSS_ZPG c0cmrdec_g3_zpg

.subckt c0cmrdec_g3_zpg 
MA3H_RDP MA3H_WRP MA3L_RDP MA3L_WRP MA012<0> MA012<1> MA012<2> MA012<3> MA012<4> MA012<5> MA456<1> MA456<4> MA456<6> MA456<7> MR16WPB MR16WPD MR17RP MR17WP MR18RP MR18WP MR21RPB MR21RPD MR64RPB MR64RPD MR65RPB MR65RPD MR105WPB MR105WPD MR106RPB MR106RPD MR107RPB MR107RPD MR108RPB MR108RPD MR120RPB MR120RPD MR121WPB MR121WPD MR122RPB MR122RPD MRGRDP MR_AUTOMOTIVE_EN MR_CATENB MR_CSTENB VPERI VPERI_ZPG VSS VSS_ZPG
-> SPICE NETLIST 는 연결 순서만 본다. MA456<3> <-> MA456<4>가 잘못 연결됨.

GPT는 이렇게 찾는다
✅ 최종 요약 (핵심만)
잘못 연결된 것 2개

MA456<3> ↔ MA456<4>
→ 비트 index mismatch (명백한 오류)
MRG3RDP ↔ MRGRDP
→ pin 의미 불일치 (오타 or 신호 혼선)

그럼 MCP Server에서 따로 Labeling만 진행해서 찾으면 될 수도?

큰 사이즈의 넷리스트
